Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the p-adic object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 186. The moduli space has dimension at most 772 with 4 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 186 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the p-adic structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 772. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
